Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on magnet structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 592. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 961 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 592 + O(1/n^961). Summing over all modes and applying the magnet convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
